| 18 | 12 |  |  | DAM3-05-011-SZS-TS9-1-HSF-SOR-0009-00 | An electrical distribution panel was observed installed outdoors along the site perimeter walkway in an unsafe condition. The enclosure door was missing/open, leaving live electrical components including busbars, circuit breakers, and unterminated wiring exposed to accidental human contact and environmental conditions. The panel was mounted on an improvised freestanding metal frame and lacked a weatherproof/IP-rated enclosure, lockable access provision, and required electrical hazard warning signage. In addition, multiple power and control cables were routed to the panel with cables lying directly on the sand without adequate mechanical protection, cable trays, trenching, or elevation.
